Documentos · Design

Fonte: design/tripo/metamorfo-roteiro-tripo.md

Metamorfo: roteiro Tripo do nível 1 ao 150, mais a Fera de cristal (copia-e-cola)

Status: roteiro pronto para colar, 19/11. Nada foi gerado. Autor: Claude (art-director).
Modelo seguido: design/tripo/barbaro-cadeia-armaduras-1-150.md (tabela de níveis, fluxo, frases de abertura e fecho), com os ajustes do criador de 19/11 (ver §0).
Fontes: design/gdd/metamorfo.md (§3.2 a 3.8), design/gdd/class-tree-v2.md §1.12, design/narrative/historias-das-classes-v2.md §8, design/art/visual-equipamento-por-classe.md (§3, §9, §10, §11, §12), design/art/cores-classes-distancia-2026-11-19.md.
Entrada: só imagens nossas. Nunca usar asset da Asset Store ou do Fab como entrada.
Alinhado ao PILOTO APROVADO (19/11, "ficou demais, muito bom"; prompt 1a): guerreiro feral meio tomado pela fera, alto, atlético e forte, postura de quem espreita, olhos âmbar, barba rala, cabelo escuro preso, pintura tribal verde; braço ESQUERDO de fera do ombro para baixo (pelo escuro, cristais e garras violeta) que cresce com o nível; braço direito humano que evolui em armadura; manto curto lima até o 40, capa/manto aberto do 41 em diante (nunca vestido); colete de couro, cinto com chocalhos de osso, calça de couro com faixas de pelo, botas; cajado em gancho com chocalhos.
- Bloco de identidade reescrito (versões HOMEM e MULHER) com o Forbidden do criador: saia, manto longo, vestido-sino, batina, padre/monge, gorro de pastor, corpo magro, os dois braços humanos.
- Níveis 1-37: roupa reescrita a partir do piloto (nível 1 = a roupa do piloto). 41-150: saias viraram calça + tassets curtos, casacos viraram hip-length, capas abertas atrás; gorro trocado por cabelo preso; braço de fera nunca coberto; linha ARMS por nível (cristal e garras crescem por série).
- MULHER: coque alto, nada caindo nos ombros, e "Keep her feminine cut." no fim. Fundo novo em todo corpo: cinza neutro liso #7A7A7A, luz frontal suave.
- Mantidos: IGNORE/COMPLETELY NEW level N, REDESIGNED for level N, CRYSTAL TOUCH, SHOWPIECE, ITEM carvão. A Fera (§7) não mudou. Nada foi gerado além do piloto.

Resumo

  1. Metamorfo (Ironroot, família Roupão): lima-líquen #A4C639 dominante + turfa #4A3B2A + cobre #B87333 (chocalhos); cristal violeta #8E5BD0 só de acento, só no braço esquerdo (no humano) e no corpo da Fera. Terceiro tom claro: creme #E8DFC8. Sem chapéu: cabelo escuro preso (mulher de coque alto); chifre de cristal na têmpora esquerda a partir da S4. Assinatura: braço esquerdo de fera (pelo, cristal e garras violeta), que cresce a cada nível.
  2. Arma: cajado de pastor em gancho com chocalhos (cobre). Na Fera não há arma: as garras de cristal herdam o refino.
  3. Cadeia: 74 designs (8 únicos até o 37 + 33 níveis x 2 ramos do 41 ao 150). A = Guarda-Rebanho (lã, sinos, rebanho de cristal domado); B = Fera-Brava (cristal à mostra, garras, crista de lã).
  4. Níveis 1 a 37: 3 prompts por nível (HOMEM, MULHER, ARMA), todos partindo do corpo base e repetindo o bloco de IDENTIDADE. Níveis 41 a 150 (modelo de ramos): por nível, 1 ITEM (a armadura neutra, sozinha, fundo carvão), HOMEM-A, HOMEM-B, MULHER-A e MULHER-B (o mesmo item interpretado por ramo) e 1 ARMA única. Nenhum depende de armadura anterior. Pose T e Multivista pelos moldes da §0. Pose T e Multivista pelos moldes da §0. Seção 7: a Fera de cristal (modelo próprio, quadrúpede, rig "Outro" no Tripo, animações próprias).
  5. Fim do arquivo: a Arte de apresentação (KEY ART e RETRATO, níveis 50 e 150) e os 2 prompts fixos de arte do item (fundo carvão). Só o nível 150 tem brilho sutil no traje; o cristal do Metamorfo (braço e Fera) pode ter um brilho interno leve, como no GDD.

0. Como usar

Fluxo de cada nível: REFERÊNCIA primeiro.

  1. HOMEM (nó "Gerar imagem"). MÉTODO NOVO (criador, 19/11): TODO nível (chave e intermediária) parte do CORPO BASE masculino em pose T, e o prompt manda ignorar a roupa atual ("IGNORE the current clothes and dress the character in a COMPLETELY NEW level N outfit: ...") descrevendo a roupa inteira do zero. A continuidade vem da paleta e dos temas do texto, não da imagem anterior.
  2. MULHER (nó "Gerar imagem"). Entrada = corpo base feminino em pose T, mais a referência HOMEM aprovada deste nível como guia de design. Mesmo método. ARMA: parte da arma do nível anterior (imagem da arma aprovada) e é redesenhada (cabeça e cabo novos).
  3. ARMA: da referência, "Extração de elementos" (só o cajado) e depois "Gerar imagem" para endireitar.
  4. ARMADURA (homem e mulher): template Pose T com o molde abaixo, depois Multivista. 3D só com o OK e o custo.

Molde POSE T (colar sobre cada referência aprovada): `` Same character and exact same outfit as the reference, staff removed, front view, symmetric T-pose, arms open, palms down, empty hands, full body. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. ` Molde MULTIVISTA (sobre a pose T aprovada): ` Same character, front, back and side views of the exact same outfit in T-pose, empty hands; the back shows the same pieces as the front. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. ``

Regras de design (as do Bárbaro, com os ajustes do criador de 19/11):

  • Paleta: lima #A4C639 em 45-55% (saturação >= 65%, valor alto, para não virar o verde do Caçador), creme #E8DFC8 como tom claro (golas, punhos, barras), turfa #4A3B2A secundária (nunca a maior mancha), cobre #B87333 acento. Violeta só no braço esquerdo (e nos chifres).
  • Silhueta (piloto aprovado): guerreiro feral alto e forte, manto curto, colete, calça de couro com faixas de pelo, botas, cajado em gancho acima da cabeça. Nunca: saia, manto longo, vestido-sino, batina, padre/monge, gorro de pastor, corpo magro, os dois braços humanos, folhas de druida.
  • CAPA ou MANTO só a partir da S5 (nível 41+); antes, no máximo uma gola pequena de feltro. Ornamento e crânios só da S4 em diante. S2 e S3 são novas e diferentes, mas sóbrias. A curva de poder sobe aos poucos.
  • Cada CHAVE muda MUITO (silhueta nova, botas novas, braçadeiras novas, arma redesenhada), legível a 10 m, e parte do CORPO BASE em pose T.
  • Cada INTERMEDIÁRIA SUBSTITUI >= 3 peças com desenho novo (colete ou calça, botas, braçadeiras ou ombro; nunca só textura ou cor), com "REPLACE ... with ...", "NEW boots: ...", "NEW bracers: ...". Na ARMA, todo nível redesenha cabeça E cabo (mesma linha, mas visivelmente outro item).
  • Nada de ombro ou braço à mostra forçado, nem assimetria forçada. Do 41 em diante o traje cobre tudo, estilo Diablo IV. O braço esquerdo de fera é a assinatura da classe: do ombro para baixo, pelo escuro, cristal e garras violeta, sempre à vista e maior a cada série; o direito é humano e ganha armadura.
  • Brilho: traje fosco, sem emissão; só o cristal violeta pode ter um brilho interno muito leve (veias). O nível 150 tem o único brilho sutil de traje.
  • Feminino: mesmo design e paleta, coque alto (nada caindo nos ombros), corte feminino real (colete moldado ao busto, cintura bem marcada, calça justa e bota alta; volume nas peças, nunca "o homem afinado").
  • Regra final do criador (19/11): todo nível troca peças E materiais. Túnica/barra do casaco, botas e braçadeiras têm desenho e material novos em todo nível (chave e intermediária); nunca repetir a mesma peça entre níveis. É a mesma classe em metamorfose.
  • Frente e costas com as mesmas peças. Nunca mais leve que a anterior. Prova: miniatura em cinza ao lado da anterior.
  • Ordem: todas as chaves partem do corpo base e rodam em paralelo; cada intermediária espera só a sua chave; A e B em paralelo do 41 em diante.

BLOCO DE IDENTIDADE DA CLASSE (vai em TODO prompt de HOMEM e MULHER, do 1 ao 150; alinhado ao piloto aprovado em 19/11)

Traços desenháveis: guerreiro feral meio tomado pela fera, alto, atlético e forte, postura de quem espreita; assinatura = braço esquerdo de fera do ombro para baixo (pelo escuro, cristais violeta, garras violeta longas), que cresce a cada nível (mais cristal, garras maiores); braço direito humano, que evolui em armadura. Manto curto lima até o 40; capa/manto do 41 em diante, sempre aberto atrás, nunca vestido; calça de couro com faixas de pelo e botas sempre à vista. Cabelo escuro preso (mulher: coque alto, nada caindo nos ombros). Cor: lima vivo dominante, turfa e cobre, violeta só no braço de fera. Forbidden: saia, manto longo, vestido-sino, batina, padre/monge, gorro de pastor, corpo magro, os dois braços humanos. HOMEM (na MULHER o rosto vira "a fierce feminine face with amber eyes, dark hair in a high top-knot bun (nothing falling on the shoulders)" e o corpo "tall, athletic and strong"): `` METAMORPH IDENTITY: a feral warrior half-claimed by the beast, NOT a priest, NOT a monk, NOT a druid: tall, athletic and powerful with broad shoulders, a low prowling stance, head slightly forward, fierce amber eyes, a rugged scruffy face with a short wild beard, messy dark hair tied back, green tribal markings on the cheek. SIGNATURE: the LEFT arm is a beast arm from the shoulder down, dark fur with matte violet crystal #8E5BD0 growths and long violet crystal claws, bigger and more crystal every level; the RIGHT arm stays human and muscular and is armored more richly every level. Silhouette: a lime mantle or cape over the shoulders, a fitted leather jerkin or armored vest, a belt with bone rattles and pouches, fitted leather trousers with fur bands at the shins, sturdy boots, the legs always visible, and a hooked shepherd staff with rattles rising above the head. COLOR CONTRAST, readable at 10 m: vivid saturated lichen lime #A4C639 is the dominant and brightest color, peat brown #4A3B2A and copper #B87333 second, cream #E8DFC8 and grey fur as light trims, violet crystal only on the beast arm (and the temple horns and the small tamed crystal pieces the outfit names); matte materials, crisp color blocks. FORBIDDEN: skirt, long robe, bell-shaped dress or cassock, priest or monk look, shepherd hat or cap, skinny or frail body, both arms human, druid leaves, vines or antlers, forest green or olive (the Hunter's colors), violet or purple clothing, an all-brown or all-green monochrome outfit, glowing cloth, cartoon look. ``

1. Conceito do piloto masculino S1

1a. Conceito (UM conceito por nó, texto no singular; o criador varia pela quantidade de imagens do nó; pode ter arma): `` ONE single full-body character concept of a male fantasy Shapeshifter (beast-bonded wild warrior), one figure only, large and centered, high-end action RPG hero concept art in the same painterly 3D style as a rugged barbarian hero, wild and unforgettable at first sight. A feral hunter half-claimed by the beast: tall, athletic and powerful with broad shoulders (NOT frail, NOT a priest), a low prowling stance, head slightly forward, fierce amber eyes, a rugged scruffy face with a short wild beard, messy dark hair tied back, green tribal markings on the cheek. SIGNATURE: his LEFT arm is transformed into a beast arm from the shoulder down, covered in dark fur with violet crystal growths along the forearm and long violet crystal claws; the right arm stays human and muscular. Level 1 starter gear, simple but iconic: a short lime-green hooded mantle of thick felted wool lined with grey fur over the shoulders (hood down), a fitted sleeveless leather jerkin laced at the sides, a wide belt with small bone rattles and pouches, fitted leather trousers with fur wraps at the shins, sturdy boots, leather bracer on the right arm. He holds a tall hooked shepherd staff of dark wood with bone rattles tied under the hook. Lime green #A4C639 dominant and vivid, peat brown and copper second, violet crystal only on the beast arm, strong readable contrast. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. FORBIDDEN: skirt, long robe, bell-shaped dress or cassock, priest or monk look, shepherd hat, skinny or frail body, both arms human, glowing parts, cartoon look. ` 1b. Pose T do escolhido (corpo-base masculino): ` Same Metamorph as the chosen concept, same face, same dark hair tied back, same beast LEFT arm (dark fur, violet crystal growths, long violet crystal claws) and human right arm, same exact level 1 outfit (short lime hooded mantle lined with grey fur, sleeveless leather jerkin, rattle belt with pouches, leather trousers with fur shin wraps, boots, right leather bracer), staff removed, front view, symmetric T-pose, arms wide open, palms down, empty hands, no weapon, full body. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. ``

2. Corpo feminino base (a partir do piloto em pose T)

`` Same Metamorph as the input image turned into a woman: same level 1 outfit, palette, beast LEFT arm (dark fur, violet crystal growths, long violet crystal claws) and human right arm, same height. REAL FEMALE CUT, never a slimmed-down man: a strong athletic woman's body, a fitted sleeveless jerkin shaped to her bust, the rattle belt cinching a visibly narrow waist, long legs in fitted leather trousers with fur shin wraps and high boots, graceful neck and shoulders, a fierce feminine face with amber eyes and green tribal markings, dark hair in a high top-knot bun with nothing falling on the shoulders; volume in the short lime mantle and the fur trims. Staff removed, front view, symmetric T-pose, arms wide open, palms down, empty hands, full body. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. ``

3. Cabeças (peça separada; sem chapéu, cabelo escuro preso; mulher de coque alto)

Cabeça a partir de um corpo: `` Extract only the head and neck of the character, then: the same head alone, front view, fierce calm expression, same hair (tied back, or a high top-knot bun for the woman), no hat, no body, no shoulders, no staff, no glow. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. ` Masculinas (3): ` HEAD M1: Male Metamorph head, front view, messy dark hair tied back, a short wild beard, fierce amber eyes, green tribal markings on the cheek, no hat, no body, matte, no glow. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. ` ` HEAD M2: Male Metamorph head, front view, a thick dark braid tied back at the nape, short stubble, fierce amber eyes, two green tribal stripes across the nose, no hat, no body, matte, no glow. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. ` ` HEAD M3: Male Metamorph head, front view, dark curly hair tied back in a short tail, a rugged short beard, amber eyes, green tribal dots on the cheekbones, no hat, no body, matte, no glow. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. ` Femininas (3): ` HEAD F1: Female Metamorph head, front view, dark hair in a high top-knot bun, nothing falling on the shoulders, freckles, fierce amber eyes, green tribal markings on the cheek, no hat, no body, matte, no glow. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. ` ` HEAD F2: Female Metamorph head, front view, dark hair in a tight high bun wrapped with a peat leather cord, nothing falling on the shoulders, sharp amber eyes, two green tribal stripes, no hat, no body, matte, no glow. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. ` ` HEAD F3: Female Metamorph head, front view, dark hair in a high braided bun tied with a lime wool thread, nothing falling on the shoulders, calm amber eyes, green tribal dots on the cheekbones, no hat, no body, matte, no glow. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. ` Chifre de cristal (peça extra da S4 em diante; encaixa na têmpora esquerda de qualquer cabeça): ` Extract only the head, then: the same head with a small matte violet crystal horn growing from the left temple, pointing up and back, a very faint inner glow inside the crystal only, same hair, no hat, no body, no other glow. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. ``

4. Mãos

`` HAND M: Extract only the left beast hand of the male Metamorph, then: one open left beast hand alone, back view, dark fur over the back of the hand, matte violet crystal growths on the knuckles and long violet crystal claws, a very faint inner glow inside the crystal only, no arm, no staff. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. ` ` HAND F: Extract only the left beast hand of the female Metamorph, then: one open left beast hand alone, back view, slimmer, dark fur over the back of the hand, matte violet crystal growths on the knuckles and long violet crystal claws, a very faint inner glow inside the crystal only, no arm, no staff. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. ` ` HAND R: Extract only the right hand of the Metamorph, then: one open human right hand alone, back view, a peat leather bracer at the wrist, no arm, no staff, no glow. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. ``

5. Tabela de formas

Base Metamorfo (1) -> Pele-de-Cristal (20) -> A Guarda-Rebanho / B Fera-Brava (40) -> Pastor de Feras / Alfa de Cristal (60) -> Voz da Serra / Besta do Socalco (80) -> Pastor do Brotar / Colheita Brava (100) -> Rebanho Vivo / Fera Viva (130). Níveis com armadura: 1, 6, 11, 15, 21, 26, 31, 37 e, em A e B: 41, 44, 48, 51, 55, 60, 61, 65, 70, 71, 75, 80, 81, 85, 90, 91, 95, 100, 101, 105, 110, 111, 115, 120, 121, 125, 130, 131, 135, 140, 141, 145, 150. A = a fera é doença, domada (lã, sinos, rebanho de cristal; o braço de fera fica à vista, só preso por anéis e faixas). B = a fera é dom (cristal à mostra no antebraço e na luva, garras, crista de lã). Cobre e metais só em pinos, sinos, ganchos e fivelas.

6. Prompts por nível

Regra de entrada: todo corpo (HOMEM, MULHER, HOMEM-A/B, MULHER-A/B) parte do corpo base em pose T, em todos os níveis; a ARMA parte da arma do nível anterior e é redesenhada. Alinhamento ao piloto (19/11): todo corpo traz a IDENTIDADE do piloto e a linha ARMS; MULHER com coque alto e FEMININE CUT (1-21) ou BULK ONLY IN THE ARMOR PIECES, NOT IN HER BODY (26+); HOMEM com BULK do 26 em diante; sino-guia e cristal só crescem; 60 com VIVID ACCENTS + SHOWPIECE.

Nível 1 · S1 chave · Metamorfo (piloto)

Parte de: piloto aprovado (pose T). Marcas de longe: braço esquerdo de fera (pelo, cristais e garras violeta), manto curto lima com capuz caído e forro de pelo cinza, colete de couro sem mangas, cinto com chocalhos de osso, calça com faixas de pelo nas canelas. `` HOMEM: Same Metamorph as the input image, same face and body, same T-pose, empty hands, no weapon. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. IGNORE his current clothes and dress him in a COMPLETELY NEW level 1 outfit. METAMORPH IDENTITY: a feral warrior half-claimed by the beast, NOT a priest, NOT a monk, NOT a druid: tall, athletic and powerful with broad shoulders, a low prowling stance, head slightly forward, fierce amber eyes, a rugged scruffy face with a short wild beard, messy dark hair tied back, green tribal markings on the cheek. SIGNATURE: the LEFT arm is a beast arm from the shoulder down, dark fur with matte violet crystal #8E5BD0 growths and long violet crystal claws, bigger and more crystal every level; the RIGHT arm stays human and muscular and is armored more richly every level. Silhouette: a lime mantle or cape over the shoulders, a fitted leather jerkin or armored vest, a belt with bone rattles and pouches, fitted leather trousers with fur bands at the shins, sturdy boots, the legs always visible, and a hooked shepherd staff with rattles rising above the head. COLOR CONTRAST, readable at 10 m: vivid saturated lichen lime #A4C639 is the dominant and brightest color, peat brown #4A3B2A and copper #B87333 second, cream #E8DFC8 and grey fur as light trims, violet crystal only on the beast arm (and the temple horns and the small tamed crystal pieces the outfit names); matte materials, crisp color blocks. FORBIDDEN: skirt, long robe, bell-shaped dress or cassock, priest or monk look, shepherd hat or cap, skinny or frail body, both arms human, druid leaves, vines or antlers, forest green or olive (the Hunter's colors), violet or purple clothing, an all-brown or all-green monochrome outfit, glowing cloth, cartoon look. OUTFIT: ARMS (level 1): LEFT = the beast arm from the shoulder down, dark fur, violet crystal growths along the forearm and long violet crystal claws, matte, no glow; never sleeved, bandaged or hidden, the armor only rings or clasps it. RIGHT = human and muscular, wearing the right-arm armor described here, richer every level. dark hair tied back, green tribal markings on the cheek; a short lichen lime hooded mantle of thick felted wool over the shoulders, lined with grey fur, hood down; a fitted sleeveless peat leather jerkin laced at the sides; a wide belt with small bone rattles and pouches; fitted peat leather trousers with grey fur wraps at the shins; sturdy peat boots; a leather bracer on the right forearm; no metal yet, no long cape. Vivid lichen lime #A4C639 dominant, cream wool #E8DFC8, peat brown #4A3B2A, copper #B87333, matte, no glow. ` ` MULHER: Same Metamorph as the input image, same face and body, same T-pose, empty hands, no weapon. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. IGNORE her current clothes and dress her in a COMPLETELY NEW level 1 outfit. METAMORPH IDENTITY: a feral warrior half-claimed by the beast, NOT a priest, NOT a monk, NOT a druid: tall, athletic and strong, a low prowling stance, head slightly forward, a fierce feminine face with amber eyes, dark hair in a high top-knot bun (nothing falling on the shoulders), green tribal markings on the cheek. Silhouette: a lime mantle or cape over the shoulders, a fitted leather jerkin or armored vest, a belt with bone rattles and pouches, fitted leather trousers with fur bands at the shins, sturdy boots, the legs always visible, and a hooked shepherd staff with rattles rising above the head. COLOR CONTRAST, readable at 10 m: vivid saturated lichen lime #A4C639 is the dominant and brightest color, peat brown #4A3B2A and copper #B87333 second, cream #E8DFC8 and grey fur as light trims, violet crystal only on the beast arm (and the temple horns and the small tamed crystal pieces the outfit names); matte materials, crisp color blocks. OUTFIT: ARMS (level 1): LEFT = the beast arm from the shoulder down, dark fur, violet crystal growths along the forearm and long violet crystal claws, matte, no glow; never sleeved, bandaged or hidden, the armor only rings or clasps it. RIGHT = human and muscular, wearing the right-arm armor described here, richer every level. dark hair in a high top-knot bun, nothing falling on the shoulders, green tribal markings on the cheek; a short lichen lime hooded mantle of thick felted wool over the shoulders, lined with grey fur, hood down; a fitted sleeveless peat leather jerkin laced at the sides; a wide belt with small bone rattles and pouches; fitted peat leather trousers with grey fur wraps at the shins; sturdy peat boots; a leather bracer on the right forearm; no metal yet, no long cape. 7. A FERA de cristal (modelo próprio, não humano)

Rig e animação: a Fera é quadrúpede: no Tripo usar o rig "Outro" (quadrúpede), não o preset Mixamo humanoide. Animações próprias (andar de quatro, galope, salto/Bote, patada, mordida, derrubada, transformação 0,6 s, ocioso); os clipes humanos do Mixamo não servem. Não é peça de armadura: um corpo à parte, um só até o nível 60; do 80 em diante há variantes A e B (decisão de Claude, conforme o ramo).
Identidade (GDD §3.7): baixa e comprida, de quatro, dorso arqueado, dois chifres de cristal para trás, ~1,1 m de altura no ombro; focinho curto; sem olhos brilhando; tufo de lã lima #A4C639 no dorso (a maior mancha vista de cima); cicatriz de cristal; corpo de cristal violeta escuro fosco. Nunca: asas, rabo longo, forma de lobo realista, folhas, totem. O cristal pode ter brilho interno leve (coerente com o Instinto no GDD); tudo o mais, fosco.

Conceito (4 livres): `` Four free concepts of the Crystal Beast of a mountain shepherd: a low and long four-legged beast of dark matte violet crystal #8E5BD0, arched back, two short crystal horns swept back, short blunt muzzle, plain dark eyes that do not glow, about 1.1 meters tall at the shoulder, a thick tuft of lichen lime #A4C639 wool along the back as the biggest patch seen from above, a crystal scar line across one shoulder, not a wolf, no wings, no long tail, a very faint inner glow inside the crystal only, stylized fantasy RPG, plain grey background, four different views. ` Pose neutra (a escolhida, para o 3D e o rig quadrúpede): ` Same Crystal Beast as the chosen concept, same design, standing neutral on all four legs with the legs slightly apart, head level, mouth closed, symmetric, strict side view, then a second front view, plain grey background, neutral light, full body, no shadow baked in, very faint inner glow inside the crystal only. `` Evolução visual (cada forma parte da Fera base em pose neutra; descreve o corpo inteiro do zero):

8. Arte de apresentação (nível 50 e nível 150)

Duas versões de cada peça: nível 50 e nível 150. Como não existe armadura de nível 50, o 50 usa o traje do nível 48 (o mais próximo); o 150 usa o traje de prestígio. Escolha um ramo (A ou B) e use o HOMEM e a MULHER aprovados desse ramo como imagens de entrada (mais a ARMA aprovada do nível).

KEY ART (homem e mulher juntos, 16:9, sem texto). Entrada: HOMEM + MULHER + ARMA do nível indicado.

Nível 50 (traje do nível 48): `` KEY ART 50: Using the input images as the exact characters, designs and outfits of level 48, a wide 16:9 cinematic key art of the male Metamorph and the female Metamorph standing side by side in a heroic three-quarter pose, each holding the shepherd's crook with bells from the weapon image, the furred left beast arm with violet crystal and long claws visible, the small crystal horn at the left temple clearly visible, the matte violet Crystal Beast with its lichen lime wool tuft standing calmly at their feet, in a misty terraced mountain slope of Ironroot at dawn, dramatic rim light, palette lichen lime #A4C639, peat brown #4A3B2A and copper #B87333, painterly stylized fantasy RPG key art, matte, no glow, no text, no logo, no frame. CRYSTAL TOUCH (incredibly subtle): only one or two tiny matte pale-blue crystal flecks embedded somewhere in the gear, almost unnoticeable at first glance, no glow. ` Nível 150: ` KEY ART 150: Using the input images as the exact characters, designs and outfits of level 150, a wide 16:9 cinematic key art of the male Metamorph and the female Metamorph standing side by side in a heroic three-quarter pose, each holding the shepherd's crook with bells from the weapon image, the furred left beast arm with violet crystal and long claws visible, the small crystal horn at the left temple clearly visible, the matte violet Crystal Beast with its lichen lime wool tuft standing calmly at their feet, in a misty terraced mountain slope of Ironroot at dawn, dramatic rim light, palette lichen lime #A4C639, peat brown #4A3B2A and copper #B87333, painterly stylized fantasy RPG key art, a very subtle faint inner glow only inside the crystal and the bells, no text, no logo, no frame. CRYSTAL TOUCH (subtle): a few small discreet pale-blue crystal inlays with a faint soft inner glow, a quiet accent only, never dominant. ``

RETRATO (busto, 1:1). Entrada: o HOMEM ou a MULHER aprovado do nível; rodar uma vez para cada um.

9. Os 2 prompts FIXOS de arte do item (fundo carvão)

Idênticos aos dos outros roteiros; nunca editar. Entrada: a imagem da ITEM (armadura) ou da ARMA aprovada do nível.

Fixo 1: ARMADURA (arte do item): `` The full torso armor from the input image displayed alone as a game item, including ALL its parts: collar or cape, both pauldrons, chest pieces and straps, belt and the thigh tassets. No boots, no bracers, no gloves, no arms, no body. Front view, centered, plain dark charcoal background, soft studio light from the top-left, subtle rim light, high detail. ``

Fixo 2: ARMA (arte do item): `` The same weapon from the input image displayed alone as a game item: identical design, materials and colors. Diagonal angle from bottom-left to top-right, centered, plain dark charcoal background, soft studio light from the top-left, subtle rim light, high detail, no hands, no text. ``
